	try {
		let pathSegments = [];
		try {
			pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split("/").filter((segment) => segment !== "" && segment !== "global").slice(0, 2);
		} catch {
			pathSegments = [];
		}
		const matchedLang = pathSegments.map((seg) => validLangs.find((lang) => lang.toLowerCase() === seg.toLowerCase())).find((lang) => lang !== void 0);
		const candidates = [];
		if (matchedLang) candidates.push(matchedLang);
		else candidates.push(defaultLang);
		candidates.push("global");
		let content = void 0;
		let usedLang = void 0;
		for (const key of candidates) {
			if (!key) continue;
			const banner = bannerByLocale[key];
			if (!banner?.content) continue;
			content = banner.content;
			usedLang = key;
			break;
		}
		if (!content) {
			document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
			return;
		}
		let shouldShow = true;
		let i = 0;
		while (i < localStorage.length) {
			const key = localStorage.key(i);
			i++;
			if (!key?.endsWith(bannerDismissedKey)) continue;
			const langSpecificDismissal = localStorage.getItem(key);
			if (!!langSpecificDismissal && langSpecificDismissal === content) {
				shouldShow = false;
				break;
				//!key.startsWith("lang:")) makes this backwards compatible with removing legacy local storage
			} else if (usedLang && (key.startsWith(`lang:${usedLang}_`) || !key.startsWith("lang:"))) {
				localStorage.removeItem(key);
				i--;
			}
		}
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, shouldShow ? "visible" : "hidden");
	} catch (e) {
		console.error(e);
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
	}
})(
  {},
  "en",
  [],
  "data-banner-state",
  "bannerDismissed",
)</script><style>:root {

	function setIsOpaque() {
		const offset = 50;
		let isOpaque = false;
		if (window.scrollY > offset) isOpaque = true;
		else isOpaque = false;
		const el = document.getElementById(navbarTransitionId);
		if (!el || !el.hasAttribute("data-is-opaque")) return;
		el.setAttribute("data-is-opaque", `${!!isOpaque}`);
	}
	/** https://github.com/pacocoursey/next-themes/blob/a7eeabc39cfb37d74ea3d82eac674d0e1851b1cb/next-themes/src/index.tsx#L234-L253 */
	function disableAnimation() {
		const css = document.createElement("style");
		const scope = `#${navbarTransitionId}`;
		css.appendChild(document.createTextNode(`${scope},${scope} *,${scope} *::before,${scope} *::after{-webkit-transition:none!important;-moz-transition:none!important;-o-transition:none!important;-ms-transition:none!important;transition:none!important}`));
		document.head.appendChild(css);
		return function() {
			window.getComputedStyle(document.body);
			setTimeout(() => {
				document.head.removeChild(css);
			}, 1);
		};
	}
	const restoreAnimation = disableAnimation();
	function done() {
		window.removeEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque);
		document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
		setIsOpaque();
		restoreAnimation();
	}
	window.addEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque, { passive: true });
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
	("requestAnimationFrame" in globalThis ? requestAnimationFrame : setTimeout)(setIsOpaque);
	if (document.readyState === "complete") done();
	else window.addEventListener("load", done, { once: true });
})("navbar-transition")</script><script id="_mintlify-scroll-margin-script">(function () {
  var measure = function measureScrollMarginTop() {
	const chromeElements = document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]");
	let chromeBottom = 0;
	chromeElements.forEach((element) => {
		const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
		if (rect.height > 0) chromeBottom = Math.max(chromeBottom, rect.bottom);
	});
	let containerTop = 0;
	const contentContainer = document.getElementById("content-container");
	if (contentContainer) {
		const { overflowY } = getComputedStyle(contentContainer);
		if (overflowY === "auto" || overflowY === "scroll") containerTop = Math.max(0, contentContainer.getBoundingClientRect().top);
	}
	const scrollMt = `${Math.round(Math.max(0, chromeBottom - containerTop)) + 40}px`;
	const rootStyle = document.documentElement.style;
	if (rootStyle.getPropertyValue("--scroll-mt") !== scrollMt) rootStyle.setProperty("--scroll-mt", scrollMt);
};
  (function watchScrollMarginTop(measure) {
	let observed = [];
	let frame = 0;
	const resizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined" ? null : new ResizeObserver(schedule);
	function run() {
		const elements = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]"));
		if ((elements.length !== observed.length || elements.some((element, index) => element !== observed[index])) && resizeObserver) {
			resizeObserver.disconnect();
			elements.forEach((element) => resizeObserver.observe(element));
		}
		observed = elements;
		measure();
	}
	function schedule() {
		if (frame) return;
		frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			frame = 0;
			run();
		});
	}
	const chromeSelector = "header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]";
	function touchesChrome(mutation) {
		const target = mutation.target;
		if (target instanceof Element && target.closest(chromeSelector)) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.addedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.removedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		return false;
	}
	new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
		if (mutations.some(touchesChrome)) schedule();
	}).observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	window.addEventListener("resize", schedule);
	if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", run);
	run();
})(measure);
  (function pinInitialHash(measure) {
	const hash = window.location.hash.slice(1);
	if (!hash) return;
	function isSmooth() {
		return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).scrollBehavior === "smooth";
	}
	function pin(behavior) {
		const target = document.getElementById(hash);
		if (!target) return false;
		measure();
		target.scrollIntoView(behavior ? { behavior } : void 0);
		return true;
	}
	if (document.readyState === "complete") {
		pin();
		return;
	}
	const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
		if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
	});
	function stop() {
		observer.disconnect();
		window.removeEventListener("load", onLoad);
	}
	function settle() {
		const inputEvents = [
			"wheel",
			"touchstart",
			"keydown",
			"pointerdown"
		];
		let ticks = 0;
		let lastY = window.scrollY;
		const interval = setInterval(() => {
			ticks += 1;
			const target = document.getElementById(hash);
			if (!target || ticks >= 6) return done();
			const scrolling = Math.abs(window.scrollY - lastY) > 1;
			lastY = window.scrollY;
			if (scrolling) return;
			const margin = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(target).scrollMarginTop) || 0;
			if (Math.abs(target.getBoundingClientRect().top - margin) > 4) pin();
		}, 350);
		function done() {
			clearInterval(interval);
			inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.removeEventListener(event, done));
		}
		inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.addEventListener(event, done, { passive: true }));
	}
	function onLoad() {
		const smooth = isSmooth();
		stop();
		if (smooth) requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			pin();
			settle();
		});
	}
	window.addEventListener("load", onLoad);
	observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
})(measure);
})();</script><a href="#content-area" class="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:top-2 focus:left-2 focus:z-50 focus:p-2 focus:text-sm focus:bg-background-light dark:focus:bg-background-dark focus:rounded-md focus:outline-primary dark:focus:outline-primary-light">Skip to main content</a><script>(function assistantScriptFn(storageKey, widthKey, cssVar, hasChatPermissions, isAssistantUiV2) {
  var defaultWidth = 368;
  try {
    if (isAssistantUiV2) {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, '0px');
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', 'closed');
      return;
    }
    var isMobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1024px)').matches;
    if (isMobile || !hasChatPermissions) {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, '0px');
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', 'closed');
      if (!hasChatPermissions) localStorage.setItem(storageKey, 'false');
      return;
    }
    var raw = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (raw === null) {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, '0px');
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', 'closed');
      return;
    }
    var isOpen = JSON.parse(raw);
    var widthRaw = localStorage.getItem(widthKey);
    var width = widthRaw !== null ? JSON.parse(widthRaw) : defaultWidth;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, isOpen ? width + 'px' : '0px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', isOpen ? 'open' : 'closed');
  } catch (e) {
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, '0px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', 'closed');
  }
})(

  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content') && matches[j].getClientRects().length) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var row = item.querySelector(':scope > button') || item;
    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

xl:w-[calc(100%-28rem)] lg:max-w-[calc(var(--content-width,100vw)+5.95rem)]" data-empty-page-layout="" id="content-area" data-layout-item="content"><header id="header" data-layout-item="page-header" group-hover:text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-600 dark:group-hover:text-gray-400 shrink-0 rotate-90"><use href="#mi-mint-1-ChevronRight"></use></svg></button></div></header><div class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Resources" data-page-href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources" id="content"><div id="enable-section-numbers"></div>
<span data-as="p">The Model Context Protocol (MCP) provides a standardized way for servers to expose
resources to clients. Resources allow servers to share data that provides context to
language models, such as files, database schemas, or application-specific information.
Each resource is uniquely identified by a
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc3986" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">URI</a>.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutNote"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">For brevity, the request examples on this page omit the <code>_meta</code> request
metadata (<code>io.modelcontextprotocol/protocolVersion</code>,
<code>io.modelcontextprotocol/clientInfo</code>, and
<code>io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities</code>). Every request <strong>MUST</strong> include
the required <code>_meta</code> fields; see
<a href="/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index#meta" class="link"><code>_meta</code></a>.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="user-interaction-model"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#user-interaction-model" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">User Interaction Model</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Resources in MCP are designed to be <strong>application-driven</strong>, with host applications
determining how to incorporate context based on their needs.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, applications could:</span>
<ul>
<li>Expose resources through UI elements for explicit selection, in a tree or list view</li>
<li>Allow the user to search through and filter available resources</li>
<li>Implement automatic context inclusion, based on heuristics or the AI model’s selection</li>
</ul>
<span data-ramka-trigger="" class="zoom-image-trigger contents cursor-zoom-in"><span class="contents"><picture class="contents"><img data-path="specification/2026-07-28/server/resource-picker.png" <span data-as="p">However, implementations are free to expose resources through any interface pattern that
suits their needs—the protocol itself does not mandate any specific user
interaction model.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="capabilities"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#capabilities" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Capabilities</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Servers that support resources <strong>MUST</strong> declare the <code>resources</code> capability:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="8" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7l8n5deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:auto;background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="json"><code language="json" numberOfLines="8"><span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;capabilities&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    &quot;resources&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      &quot;listChanged&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      &quot;subscribe&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The capability supports two optional features:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>listChanged</code>: whether the server will emit notifications when the list of available
resources changes.</li>
<li><code>subscribe</code> : whether the server supports resource-specific update notifications
for resources requested through subscriptions/listen using the resourceSubscriptions
filter.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Servers may advertise either feature independently, together or neither.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers that support neither <code>listChanged</code> nor <code>subscribe</code> may omit it:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="5" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7l915deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md group/code-snippet-feedback-button [.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-100 dark:[.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-800 [.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent dark:[.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent" id="code-snippet-feedback-button" aria-label="Report incorrect code"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 shrink-0 text-gray-400 group-hover/code-snippet-feedback-button:text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40 dark:group-hover/code-snippet-feedback-button:text-white/60"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Report"></use></svg></button></div><div class="code-block-copy-button z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7p915deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md group/copy-button [.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-100 dark:[.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-800 [.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent dark:[.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent" data-testid="copy-code-button" aria-label="Copy the contents from the code block"><svg aria-hidden="true" href="#mi-mint-1-Copy"></use></svg></button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></div><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7t915deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md group/ask-assistant-button [.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-100 dark:[.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-800 [.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent dark:[.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent" id="ask-assistant-code-block-button" aria-label="Ask Assistant" data-chat-payload-element-id="lang-json-code-_____capabilities___"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 shrink-0 text-gray-400 group-hover/ask-assistant-button:text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40 dark:group-hover/ask-assistant-button:text-white/60"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Sparkle"></use></svg></button></div></div><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:auto;background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-2xl bg-white"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_6915deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="json"><code language="json" numberOfLines="5"><span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;capabilities&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    &quot;resources&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Servers that declare the <code>resources</code> capability <strong>MUST</strong> respond to <code>resources/list</code>
requests with the set of resources currently available to the requesting client. This set
<strong>MAY</strong> be empty and <strong>MAY</strong> change over time (see
<a href="#list-changed-notification" class="link">List Changed Notification</a>), but <strong>MUST NOT</strong> vary
per-connection or as a side effect of other requests on the connection. The set
<strong>MAY</strong> vary by the authorization presented on the request — for example, returning
only the resources the caller’s granted scopes permit — since credentials are
per-request input, not connection state.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="protocol-messages"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#protocol-messages" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Protocol Messages</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="listing-resources"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#listing-resources" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Listing Resources</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To discover available resources, clients send a <code>resources/list</code> request. This operation
supports <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination" class="link">pagination</a> and <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching" class="link">caching</a>.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="reading-resources"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#reading-resources" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Reading Resources</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To retrieve resource contents, clients send a <code>resources/read</code> request. This operation
supports <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching" class="link">caching</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Request:</strong></span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;id&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">2</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;result&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    &quot;resultType&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;complete&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">      {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        &quot;uri&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;file:///project/src/main.rs&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">      }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    &quot;cacheScope&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;private&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>MAY</strong> return multiple resource contents in response to a single
<code>resources/read</code> request. For example, a server could return the contents of
several files when a directory resource is read.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>MAY</strong> also respond to <code>resources/read</code> with an <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/mrtr#inputrequiredresult" class="link"><code>InputRequiredResult</code></a> to indicate that additional input is needed before the resource can be read. This follows the <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/mrtr#multi-round-trip-requests" class="link">multi round-trip requests</a> mechanism. When retrying the request, clients include <code>inputResponses</code> and, if provided by the server, <code>requestState</code> in the request parameters.</span>
<span data-as="p">Alternatively, if the scheme of <code>uri</code> is <code>https://</code>, clients may fetch the resource directly from the web. See the <a href="#https%3A%2F%2F" class="link">Common URI Schemes section</a> for more information.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="resource-templates"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#resource-templates" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Resource Templates</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Resource templates allow servers to expose parameterized resources using
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6570" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">URI templates</a>. Arguments may be
auto-completed through <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion" class="link">the completion API</a>.
This operation supports <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination" class="link">pagination</a> and <a href="/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching" class="link">caching</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Request:</strong></span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="8" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7lab5deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-2xl bg-white"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_6ab5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki <h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="subscriptions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#subscriptions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Subscriptions</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Clients subscribe to change notifications for specific resources by sending a
<a href="/specification/2026-07-28/schema#subscriptionslistenrequest" class="link"><code>subscriptions/listen</code></a> request with the resource URIs listed in
<code>notifications.resourceSubscriptions</code>. The server delivers
<code>notifications/resources/updated</code> on the resulting stream whenever a watched resource
changes.</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="message-flow"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#message-flow" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Message Flow</span></h2>
<!-- -->
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="data-types"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#data-types" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Data Types</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="resource"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#resource" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Resource</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">A resource definition includes:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>uri</code>: Unique identifier for the resource</li>
<li><code>name</code>: The name of the resource.</li>
<li><code>title</code>: Optional human-readable name of the resource for display purposes.</li>
<li><code>description</code>: Optional description</li>
<li><code>icons</code>: Optional array of icons for display in user interfaces</li>
<li><code>mimeType</code>: Optional MIME type</li>
<li><code>size</code>: Optional size in bytes</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="resource-contents"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#resource-contents" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Resource Contents</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Resources can contain either text or binary data:</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="text-content"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#text-content" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Text Content</span></h4>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="annotations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#annotations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Annotations</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Resources, resource templates and content blocks support optional annotations that provide hints to clients about how to use or display the resource:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>audience</code></strong>: An array indicating the intended audience(s) for this resource. Valid values are <code>&quot;user&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;assistant&quot;</code>. For example, <code>[&quot;user&quot;, &quot;assistant&quot;]</code> indicates content useful for both.</li>
<li><strong><code>priority</code></strong>: A number from 0.0 to 1.0 indicating the importance of this resource. A value of 1 means “most important” (effectively required), while 0 means “least important” (entirely optional).</li>
<li><strong><code>lastModified</code></strong>: An ISO 8601 formatted timestamp indicating when the resource was last modified (e.g., <code>&quot;2025-01-12T15:00:58Z&quot;</code>).</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Example resource with annotations:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">Clients can use these annotations to:</span>
<ul>
<li>Filter resources based on their intended audience</li>
<li>Prioritize which resources to include in context</li>
<li>Display modification times or sort by recency</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="common-uri-schemes"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#common-uri-schemes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Common URI Schemes</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The protocol defines several standard URI schemes. This list is not
exhaustive—implementations are always free to use additional, custom URI schemes.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="https//"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#https//" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">https://</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Used to represent a resource available on the web.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> use this scheme only when the client is able to fetch and load the
resource directly from the web on its own—that is, it doesn’t need to read the resource
via the MCP server.</span>
<span data-as="p">For other use cases, servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> prefer to use another URI scheme, or define a
custom one, even if the server will itself be downloading resource contents over the
internet.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="file//"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#file//" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">file://</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Used to identify resources that behave like a filesystem. However, the resources do not
need to map to an actual physical filesystem.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MAY</strong> identify file:// resources with an
<a href="https://specifications.freedesktop.org/shared-mime-info-spec/0.14/ar01s02.html#id-1.3.14" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">XDG MIME type</a>,
like <code>inode/directory</code>, to represent non-regular files (such as directories) that don’t
otherwise have a standard MIME type.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="git//"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#git//" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">git://</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Git version control integration.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="custom-uri-schemes"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#custom-uri-schemes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Custom URI Schemes</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Custom URI schemes <strong>MUST</strong> be in accordance with <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc3986" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC3986</a>,
taking the above guidance in to account.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="error-handling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#error-handling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Error Handling</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">If the requested resource does not exist, servers <strong>MUST</strong> return a JSON-RPC error with
code <code>-32602</code> (Invalid Params). Servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> return <code>-32603</code> for internal errors.</span>
<span data-as="p">For backwards compatibility, clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> also accept <code>-32002</code> as a
resource not found error, as earlier protocol versions used this code.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>MUST NOT</strong> return an empty <code>contents</code> array for a non-existent resource. An empty array is ambiguous—it could mean the resource exists but has no content, or that it doesn’t exist at all.</span>
<span data-as="p">Example error:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="11" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_7ld95deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md </code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="security-considerations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#security-considerations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Security Considerations</span></h2>
<ol>
<li>Servers <strong>MUST</strong> validate all resource URIs</li>
<li>Access controls <strong>SHOULD</strong> be implemented for sensitive resources</li>
<li>Binary data <strong>MUST</strong> be properly encoded</li>
<li>Resource permissions <strong>SHOULD</strong> be checked before operations</li>
<li>Servers <strong>MUST</strong> sanitize file paths to prevent directory traversal attacks
when serving <code>file://</code> resources</li>
</ol></div><div class="feedback-toolbar pb-16 w-full flex flex-col gap-y-8"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-4 items-center justify-between"><p class="inline-block text-sm text-gray-600
